# 牵头者人物作用域；输入tca_fs_record、goal_index及prepare输出的saved scopes，调用者负责授权和清理。
# 仅1—128的完整精确目标声明；无匹配数量时不建战争，不自行补目标、扣费或变更计分。
tca_fd_declare_targets_effect = {
    if = { limit = { scope:tca_fd_goal_index = 128 }
        start_war = {
            cb = tca_frontier_recovery_cb
            target = scope:tca_fs_record.var:tca_fd_enemy
            target_title = scope:tca_fd_target_1
